'use strict';

// ========== SETTINGS ==========
function openSettings() {
  closeSidebar();
  renderSettingsSwatches();
  document.getElementById('poster-bg-toggle').checked = settings.posterBg;
  injectSnowToggle();
  if (typeof injectDismissedReset === 'function') injectDismissedReset();
  document.getElementById('settings-modal').classList.add('open');
}

function closeSettings() { 
  document.getElementById('settings-modal').classList.remove('open'); 
}

function onPosterBgToggle(on) {
  settings.posterBg = on;
  localStorage.setItem('mycinema.posterBg', on ? 'true' : 'false');
  if (typeof touchAppearance === 'function') touchAppearance();
  if (cardStack.length) renderCard();
}

function restartOnboardingFromSettings() {
  closeSettings();
  if (confirm('Пройти налаштування знову? Обрані тайтли додадуться з оцінкою 8.')) {
    localStorage.removeItem('onboardingDone');
    startOnboarding();
  }
}

function renderSettingsSwatches() {
  const bgWrap = document.getElementById('bg-swatches');
  const acWrap = document.getElementById('accent-swatches');
  bgWrap.replaceChildren();
  acWrap.replaceChildren();
  
  Object.entries(THEMES).forEach(([key, t]) => {
    const s = el('div', 'theme-swatch' + (settings.themeBg === key ? ' active' : ''));
    s.style.cssText = `width:48px;height:48px;border-radius:12px;cursor:pointer;border:3px solid ${settings.themeBg === key ? 'var(--accent)' : 'transparent'};background:linear-gradient(135deg,${t.bg1} 0%,${t.bg3} 100%);position:relative;transition:transform .15s`;
    s.innerHTML = `<span style="position:absolute;bottom:2px;left:0;right:0;text-align:center;font-size:9px;font-weight:600;color:${t.text}">${t.name}</span>`;
    s.addEventListener('click', () => { applyTheme(key, settings.themeAccent); if (typeof touchAppearance === 'function') touchAppearance(); renderSettingsSwatches(); if (cardStack.length) renderCard(); });
    bgWrap.append(s);
  });
  
  Object.entries(ACCENTS).forEach(([key, a]) => {
    const s = el('div', 'theme-swatch' + (settings.themeAccent === key ? ' active' : ''));
    s.style.cssText = `width:48px;height:48px;border-radius:12px;cursor:pointer;border:3px solid ${settings.themeAccent === key ? 'var(--accent)' : 'transparent'};background:${a.accent};position:relative;transition:transform .15s`;
    s.innerHTML = `<span style="position:absolute;bottom:2px;left:0;right:0;text-align:center;font-size:9px;font-weight:600;color:${a.fg}">${a.name}</span>`;
    s.addEventListener('click', () => { applyTheme(settings.themeBg, key); if (typeof touchAppearance === 'function') touchAppearance(); renderSettingsSwatches(); if (cardStack.length) renderCard(); });
    acWrap.append(s);
  });
}

function exportLibrary() {
  closeSettings();
  const data = JSON.stringify(lib, null, 2);
  const blob = new Blob([data], { type: 'application/json' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = 'mycinema-' + new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) + '.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  document.body.removeChild(a);
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  showSyncToast('💾 Експортовано');
}

function importLibrary() {
  closeSettings();
  const fileInput = document.getElementById('import-file');
  fileInput.value = '';
  fileInput.onchange = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    try {
      const text = await file.text();
      const data = JSON.parse(text);
      if (typeof data !== 'object' || Array.isArray(data)) throw new Error('Невірний формат');
      const count = Object.keys(data).length;
      if (!confirm('Імпортувати ' + count + ' тайтлів? Поточні буде замінено.')) return;
      const nowTs = Date.now();
      for (const k of Object.keys(lib)) if (!(k in data)) libTombstones[k] = nowTs;
      for (const k of Object.keys(data)) {
        delete libTombstones[k];
        if (data[k] && typeof data[k] === 'object') data[k].updated = nowTs;
      }
      lib = data;
      await saveNow();
      refresh();
      showSyncToast('📥 ' + count);
    } catch (err) { alert('Помилка імпорту: ' + err.message); }
  };
  fileInput.click();
}

// ========== ONBOARDING ==========
let onbSelected = new Set();
let onbCategories = [];

async function startOnboarding() {
  document.getElementById('onboarding-modal').style.display = 'flex';
  const sc = document.getElementById('onb-scroll');
  sc.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center; padding:40px; color:var(--text-muted);">Завантаження...</div>';
  onbSelected.clear();
  
  try {
    const [trM, popM, topM, trT, popT, topT] = await Promise.all([
      tmdbFetch('/trending/movie/week?language=uk-UA').catch(() => ({ results: [] })),
      tmdbFetch('/discover/movie?language=uk-UA&sort_by=popularity.desc&vote_count.gte=100&page=1').catch(() => ({ results: [] })),
      tmdbFetch('/discover/movie?language=uk-UA&sort_by=vote_average.desc&vote_count.gte=1000&page=1').catch(() => ({ results: [] })),
      tmdbFetch('/trending/tv/week?language=uk-UA').catch(() => ({ results: [] })),
      tmdbFetch('/discover/tv?language=uk-UA&sort_by=popularity.desc&vote_count.gte=100&page=1').catch(() => ({ results: [] })),
      tmdbFetch('/discover/tv?language=uk-UA&sort_by=vote_average.desc&vote_count.gte=500&page=1').catch(() => ({ results: [] }))
    ]);
    
    const dedup = (items, seen) => items.filter(x => { if (seen.has(x.key) || !x.poster) return false; seen.add(x.key); return true; });
    const seenM = new Set();
    const seenT = new Set();
    
    onbCategories = [
      { title: '🔥 Фільми · Тренди тижня', items: dedup(trM.results.map(r => normalizeItem({...r, media_type: 'movie'})), seenM).slice(0, 15) },
      { title: '⭐ Фільми · Популярне', items: dedup(popM.results.map(r => normalizeItem({...r, media_type: 'movie'})), seenM).slice(0, 20) },
      { title: '🏆 Фільми · Найкращі', items: dedup(topM.results.map(r => normalizeItem({...r, media_type: 'movie'})), seenM).slice(0, 15) },
      { title: '🔥 Серіали · Тренди тижня', items: dedup(trT.results.map(r => normalizeItem({...r, media_type: 'tv'})), seenT).slice(0, 15) },
      { title: '⭐ Серіали · Популярне', items: dedup(popT.results.map(r => normalizeItem({...r, media_type: 'tv'})), seenT).slice(0, 20) },
      { title: '🏆 Серіали · Найкращі', items: dedup(topT.results.map(r => normalizeItem({...r, media_type: 'tv'})), seenT).slice(0, 15) }
    ].filter(c => c.items.length > 0);
    
    renderOnbGrids();
  } catch (e) { sc.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align:center; padding:40px; color:var(--danger);">Помилка: ${e.message}</div>`; }
}

function renderOnbGrids() {
  const sc = document.getElementById('onb-scroll');
  sc.innerHTML = '';
  onbCategories.forEach(cat => {
    const catEl = el('div', 'onb-category');
    catEl.append(el('div', 'onb-category-title', cat.title));
    const gridEl = el('div', 'onb-grid');
    cat.items.forEach(item => {
      const c = el('div', 'onb-card' + (onbSelected.has(item.key) ? ' selected' : ''));
      const p = el('img', 'onb-poster'); p.src = TMDB_IMG + 'w185' + item.poster; p.loading = 'lazy';
      c.append(p, el('div', 'onb-title', item.title));
      c.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (onbSelected.has(item.key)) onbSelected.delete(item.key); else onbSelected.add(item.key);
        c.classList.toggle('selected'); updateOnbSubmit();
      });
      gridEl.append(c);
    });
    catEl.append(gridEl); sc.append(catEl);
  });
  updateOnbSubmit();
}

function updateOnbSubmit() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('onb-submit');
  if (onbSelected.size >= 5) { btn.classList.add('active'); btn.disabled = false; }
  else { btn.classList.remove('active'); btn.disabled = true; }
}

async function submitOnboarding() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('onb-submit');
  btn.textContent = 'Збереження...'; btn.disabled = true;
  const items = [];
  onbCategories.forEach(cat => cat.items.forEach(it => { if (onbSelected.has(it.key)) items.push(it); }));
  // [ONB-TV-FIX] Для TV — сезонні оцінки, інакше computeItemRating().avg = 0
  items.forEach(it => {
    const isTv = it.type === 'tv';
    lib[it.key] = {
      ...it,
      status: 'watched',
      ...(isTv
        ? { seasonRatings: { 1: { overall: 8 } }, totalSeasons: 1 }
        : { ratings: { overall: 8 } }),
      updated: Date.now(),
      createdAt: Date.now()
    };
  });
  localStorage.setItem('onboardingDone', 'true');
  document.getElementById('onboarding-modal').style.display = 'none';
  btn.textContent = 'Продовжити';
  await saveNow(); refresh();
}

function skipOnboarding() {
  if (confirm('Пропустити налаштування?')) {
    localStorage.setItem('onboardingDone', 'true');
    document.getElementById('onboarding-modal').style.display = 'none';
  }
}


// ========== SNOW TOGGLE (injected dynamically, no HTML in index.html) ==========
function injectSnowToggle() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('settings-modal');
  if (!modal) return;
  if (modal.querySelector('#snow-toggle')) return;

  const sections = modal.querySelectorAll('.settings-section');
  let target = null;
  sections.forEach(function (sec) {
    const title = sec.querySelector('.settings-section-title');
    if (title && /Вигляд/i.test(title.textContent)) target = sec;
  });
  if (!target) return;

  const label = document.createElement('label');
  label.className = 'settings-toggle';

  const input = document.createElement('input');
  input.type = 'checkbox';
  input.id = 'snow-toggle';
  input.checked = (typeof SnowFeature !== 'undefined') ? SnowFeature.isEnabled() : true;
  input.addEventListener('change', function () {
    if (typeof SnowFeature !== 'undefined') SnowFeature.setEnabled(this.checked);
  });

  const span = document.createElement('span');
  span.textContent = '❄️ Сніг на фоні';

  label.appendChild(input);
  label.appendChild(span);
  target.appendChild(label);
}

// ========== RESET DISMISSED ==========
function injectDismissedReset() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('settings-modal');
  if (!modal) return;
  const sections = modal.querySelectorAll('.settings-section');
  let target = null;
  sections.forEach((sec) => {
    const title = sec.querySelector('.settings-section-title');
    if (title && /Дані/i.test(title.textContent)) target = sec;
  });
  if (!target) return;

  const getCount = () => (typeof dismissedRecs === 'object' && dismissedRecs) ? Object.keys(dismissedRecs).length : 0;
  let btn = modal.querySelector('#reset-dismissed-btn');
  if (!btn) {
    btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.id = 'reset-dismissed-btn';
    btn.className = 'settings-btn';
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const cnt = getCount();
      if (!cnt) { showSyncToast('Немає схованих рекомендацій'); return; }
      if (!confirm(`Повернути всі сховані рекомендації (${cnt})?`)) return;
      if (typeof clearDismissed === 'function') clearDismissed();
      state.home.sig = null;
      try { localStorage.removeItem(RECS_CACHE); } catch {}
      btn.textContent = '🙈 Повернути сховані рекомендації (0)';
      showSyncToast('✅ Сховані рекомендації повернуто');
      if (state.tab === 'home') refresh();
    });
    target.appendChild(btn);
  }
  btn.textContent = `🙈 Повернути сховані рекомендації (${getCount()})`;
}


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  FILE: ./src/calendar.js
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

'use strict';

function renderCalendar() {
  box.style.padding = '0';
  box.replaceChildren();
  
  const wrap = el('div', 'home-wrap');
  const head = el('div', 'section-head');
  head.style.marginTop = '20px';
  head.append(el('h1', 'section-title', '📅 Календар'));
  wrap.append(head);

  const itemTs = (x) => x.statusDate || x.updated || x.createdAt || 0;
  // 1. Беремо всі тайтли з потрібними статусами й датою
  let allItems = Object.values(lib).filter(x => (x.status === 'watching' || x.status === 'watched') && itemTs(x) > 0);
  
  if (!allItems.length) {
    wrap.append(el('div', 'avail-empty', 'Тут поки порожньо. Додай фільми зі статусом "Дивлюсь" або "Переглянуто".'));
    box.append(wrap);
    return;
  }

  // 2. Фільтруємо за обраним статусом (якщо не 'all')
  let items = allItems;
  if (state.calendarStatus === 'watching') items = items.filter(x => x.status === 'watching');
  else if (state.calendarStatus === 'watched') items = items.filter(x => x.status === 'watched');

  // 3. Збираємо унікальні місяці вже з відфільтрованих елементів
  const monthsSet = new Set();
  items.forEach(item => {
    const date = new Date(itemTs(item));
    const monthKey = `${date.getFullYear()}-${date.getMonth()}`;
    monthsSet.add(monthKey);
  });
  
  const months = Array.from(monthsSet).sort((a, b) => {
    const [ya, ma] = a.split('-').map(Number);
    const [yb, mb] = b.split('-').map(Number);
    return yb - ya || mb - ma;
  });

  // Якщо поточний вибраний місяць відсутній у новому списку - беремо перший
  if (!state.calendarMonth || !monthsSet.has(state.calendarMonth)) {
    state.calendarMonth = months[0] || null;
  }

  // 4. Малюємо панель з фільтрами (Місяць + Статус)
  const calHeader = el('div', 'calendar-header');
  calHeader.style.display = 'flex';
  calHeader.style.gap = '10px';
  calHeader.style.flexWrap = 'wrap';

  // Селект статусу
  const sSelect = document.createElement('select');
  sSelect.className = 'calendar-select';
  sSelect.style.flex = '1';
  sSelect.style.minWidth = '150px';
  [
    { val: 'all', label: 'Всі статуси' },
    { val: 'watching', label: '▶️ Дивлюсь' },
    { val: 'watched', label: '✅ Переглянуто' }
  ].forEach(optData => {
    const opt = document.createElement('option');
    opt.value = optData.val;
    opt.textContent = optData.label;
    if (state.calendarStatus === optData.val) opt.selected = true;
    sSelect.append(opt);
  });
  sSelect.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
    state.calendarStatus = e.target.value;
    state.calendarMonth = null; // Скидаємо місяць, щоб він обрався автоматично з нового списку
    renderCalendar();
  });
  calHeader.append(sSelect);

  // Селект місяця
  if (months.length > 0) {
    const mSelect = document.createElement('select');
    mSelect.className = 'calendar-select';
    mSelect.style.flex = '1';
    mSelect.style.minWidth = '150px';
    
    const monthNames = ["Січень", "Лютий", "Березень", "Квітень", "Травень", "Червень", "Липень", "Серпень", "Вересень", "Жовтень", "Листопад", "Грудень"];
    
    months.forEach(m => {
      const [y, monthIdx] = m.split('-').map(Number);
      const opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = m;
      opt.textContent = `${monthNames[monthIdx]} ${y}`;
      if (m === state.calendarMonth) opt.selected = true;
      mSelect.append(opt);
    });
    
    mSelect.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      state.calendarMonth = e.target.value;
      renderCalendar();
    });
    calHeader.append(mSelect);
  }
  
  wrap.append(calHeader);

  if (!items.length) {
    wrap.append(el('div', 'avail-empty', 'Немає записів з таким статусом.'));
    box.append(wrap);
    return;
  }

  // 5. Фільтруємо по вибраному місяцю
  const filteredItems = items.filter(item => {
    const date = new Date(itemTs(item));
    return `${date.getFullYear()}-${date.getMonth()}` === state.calendarMonth;
  });

  // 5.1 Підсумок місяця
  const monthNames = ["Січень", "Лютий", "Березень", "Квітень", "Травень", "Червень", "Липень", "Серпень", "Вересень", "Жовтень", "Листопад", "Грудень"];
  const [cy, cm] = (state.calendarMonth || '').split('-').map(Number);
  let monthMins = 0;
  const monthRated = [];
  filteredItems.forEach(item => {
    if (item.type === 'movie' && item.runtime > 0) monthMins += item.runtime;
    else if (item.type === 'tv' && item.runtime > 0) {
      let eps = (item.watchedEpisodes && item.watchedEpisodes.length) || 0;
      if (item.status === 'watched' && (item.episode_count || 0) > eps) eps = item.episode_count;
      monthMins += eps * item.runtime;
    }
    const r = computeItemRating(item).avg;
    if (r > 0) monthRated.push({ item, avg: r });
  });
  const monthAvg = monthRated.length ? (monthRated.reduce((s, x) => s + x.avg, 0) / monthRated.length) : 0;
  const bestOfMonth = monthRated.length ? [...monthRated].sort((a, b) => b.avg - a.avg)[0] : null;

  const sumCard = el('div', 'insight-card cal-summary');
  sumCard.append(el('div', 'insight-label', `📊 Підсумок · ${monthNames[cm] || ''} ${cy || ''}`));
  const parts = [`<strong>${filteredItems.length}</strong> ${plural(filteredItems.length, 'тайтл', 'тайтли', 'тайтлів')}`];
  if (monthMins > 0) {
    const hrs = monthMins / 60;
    parts.push(`<strong>~${hrs >= 10 ? Math.round(hrs) : fmtNum(hrs)} год</strong>`);
  }
  if (monthAvg > 0) parts.push(`сер. оцінка <strong>${fmtNum(monthAvg)}</strong>`);
  const line = el('div', 'insight-text');
  line.innerHTML = parts.join(' · ');
  sumCard.append(line);
  if (bestOfMonth) {
    const bestBtn = el('button', 'cal-best-btn', `👑 Найкраще за місяць: ${bestOfMonth.item.title} (${fmtNum(bestOfMonth.avg)})`);
    bestBtn.type = 'button';
    bestBtn.addEventListener('click', () => openCard(bestOfMonth.item));
    sumCard.append(bestBtn);
  }
  wrap.append(sumCard);

  // 6. Групуємо за днями (YYYY-MM-DD для детермінованого сортування)
  const toDayKey = (d) => `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  const groups = {};
  filteredItems.forEach(item => {
    const date = new Date(itemTs(item));
    const dk = toDayKey(date);
    if (!groups[dk]) groups[dk] = { label: date.toLocaleDateString('uk-UA', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }), items: [] };
    groups[dk].items.push(item);
  });

  const sortedDays = Object.keys(groups).sort((a, b) => b.localeCompare(a));
  const timeline = el('div', 'timeline');

  sortedDays.forEach(dk => {
    const grp = groups[dk];
    const dayGroup = el('div', 'timeline-day-group');
    dayGroup.append(el('div', 'timeline-date', grp.label));

    const dayItems = el('div', 'timeline-items');
    grp.items.sort((a, b) => itemTs(b) - itemTs(a)).forEach(item => {
      const listItem = createListItem(item);
      const statusBadge = item.status === 'watching' ? '▶️ Дивлюсь' : '✅ Переглянуто';
      const info = listItem.querySelector('.info');
      if (info) {
        const badge = el('span', 'badge', statusBadge);
        badge.style.background = item.status === 'watching' ? 'rgba(var(--accent-rgb), 0.15)' : 'rgba(157, 229, 195, 0.15)';
        badge.style.color = item.status === 'watching' ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--mint)';
        info.insertBefore(badge, info.firstChild);
      }
      dayItems.append(listItem);
    });

    dayGroup.append(dayItems);
    timeline.append(dayGroup);
  });

  wrap.append(timeline);
  box.append(wrap);
}


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  FILE: ./src/snow.js
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

'use strict';

// Snow layer — safe: no DOM element in wheel/touch event path.
// Implementation: background-image on a pointer-events:none div.
(function () {
  const KEY = 'mycinema.snowEnabled';

  function isEnabled() {
    const v = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    return v === null ? true : v === 'true';  // default ON
  }

  function apply() {
    const existing = document.getElementById('snow-layer');
    if (isEnabled()) {
      if (!existing) {
        const layer = document.createElement('div');
        layer.id = 'snow-layer';
        layer.className = 'mcm-snow';
        layer.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        document.body.appendChild(layer);
      }
    } else if (existing) {
      existing.remove();
    }
  }

  function setEnabled(on) {
    localStorage.setItem(KEY, on ? 'true' : 'false');
    apply();
    if (typeof touchAppearance === 'function') touchAppearance();
  }

  window.SnowFeature = { isEnabled: isEnabled, setEnabled: setEnabled, apply: apply };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', apply);
  } else {
    apply();
  }
})();

const FACT_DISMISSED_KEY = 'mycinema.factDismissedDate';

function getTodayDateKey() {
  const d = new Date();
  const y = d.getFullYear();
  const m = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}-${day}`;
}

function getFactOfDay() {
  if (!CINEMA_FACTS.length) return null;
  const d = new Date();
  const dayIndex = Math.floor(Date.UTC(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate()) / 86400000);
  const idx = ((dayIndex % CINEMA_FACTS.length) + CINEMA_FACTS.length) % CINEMA_FACTS.length;
  return CINEMA_FACTS[idx];
}

function isFactDismissedToday() {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(FACT_DISMISSED_KEY) === getTodayDateKey();
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function dismissFactToday() {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(FACT_DISMISSED_KEY, getTodayDateKey());
  } catch {}
}

async function openFactMedia(fact) {
  if (!fact || !fact.query) return;
  try {
    const type = fact.type === 'tv' ? 'tv' : 'movie';
    const yParam = fact.year ? (type === 'tv' ? `&first_air_date_year=${fact.year}` : `&year=${fact.year}`) : '';
    const data = await tmdbFetch(`/search/${type}?language=uk-UA&query=${encodeURIComponent(fact.query)}${yParam}`);
    let hit = (data.results || [])[0];
    if (!hit && yParam) {
      const fallback = await tmdbFetch(`/search/${type}?language=uk-UA&query=${encodeURIComponent(fact.query)}`);
      hit = (fallback.results || [])[0];
    }
    if (hit) {
      openCard(normalizeItem({ ...hit, media_type: type }));
    } else {
      showSyncToast('Тайтл не знайдено на TMDB');
    }
  } catch (e) {
    showSyncToast('Помилка відкриття картки');
  }
}

'use strict';

function syncNavActive() {
  const t = state.tab;
  document.querySelectorAll(".nav-item").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("active", b.dataset.tab === t));
  document.querySelectorAll(".nav-group").forEach(g => {
    const hasActive = !!g.querySelector('.nav-item.active');
    g.classList.toggle('has-active', hasActive);
    if (window.matchMedia('(min-width: 900px) and (min-height: 600px)').matches) {
      g.classList.remove('open');
    } else if (hasActive) {
      g.classList.add('open');
    }
  });
  const moreGroup = document.querySelector('.nav-more-group');
  if (moreGroup) moreGroup.classList.toggle('has-active', !!moreGroup.querySelector('.nav-item.active'));
}

function setTab(t, opts) {
  state.tab = t;
  closeSidebar();
  if (!(opts && opts.keepFilter) && typeof hideFilterDropdown === 'function') hideFilterDropdown();
  if (typeof hideAutocomplete === 'function') hideAutocomplete();
  document.body.classList.toggle('no-search', !(t === 'home' || t === 'filter'));
  syncNavActive();
  typeFilterBar.classList.toggle("hidden", !["watching", "watched", "planned", "dropped", "favorites"].includes(t));
  if (t === 'game') { box.style.padding = '0'; renderGame(); return; }
  if (t === 'top') { box.style.padding = '0 16px 16px'; renderMyTop(); return; }
  refresh();
}

document.querySelectorAll(".filter-btn").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", () => setTypeFilter(b.dataset.filter)));

function setTypeFilter(f) {
  state.typeFilter = f;
  document.querySelectorAll(".filter-btn").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("active", b.dataset.filter === f));
  if (state.tab === "filter") renderFilter(); else renderLibrary();
}

function refresh() {
  if (state.tab === "home") loadHome();
  else if (state.tab === "filter") renderFilter();
  else if (state.tab === "game") renderGame();
  else if (state.tab === "statistics") renderStatistics();
  else if (state.tab === "notes") renderNotesList();
  else if (state.tab === "calendar") renderCalendar();
  else if (state.tab === "top") renderMyTop();
  else renderLibrary();
}

window.addEventListener('firebase-ready', syncFromCloud);

// ========== COLD START SPLASH (APK only, 3s) ==========
const SPLASH_POOL_KEY = 'mycinema.splashPool.v1';
const SPLASH_POOL_TTL = 12 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
const SPLASH_DURATION_MS = 3000;

function readSplashPool() {
  try {
    const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SPLASH_POOL_KEY));
    if (raw && Array.isArray(raw.items) && raw.items.length) return raw;
  } catch {}
  return null;
}

function writeSplashPool(items) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(SPLASH_POOL_KEY, JSON.stringify({ ts: Date.now(), items }));
  } catch {}
}

function initColdSplash() {
  if (document.getElementById('cold-splash')) return;

  const splash = document.createElement('div');
  splash.id = 'cold-splash';
  splash.className = 'cold-splash';

  const mediaWrap = document.createElement('div');
  mediaWrap.className = 'splash-media';

  const vignette = document.createElement('div');
  vignette.className = 'splash-vignette';

  const content = document.createElement('div');
  content.className = 'splash-content';

  const logo = document.createElement('div');
  logo.className = 'splash-logo';
  logo.textContent = 'MyCinema';

  const status = document.createElement('div');
  status.className = 'splash-status';
  status.append(document.createTextNode('Завантажуємо'));
  const dot = document.createElement('span');
  dot.className = 'splash-dot';
  dot.textContent = '.';
  status.append(dot);

  content.append(logo, status);
  splash.append(mediaWrap, vignette, content);
  document.body.appendChild(splash);

  const applyPick = (pick) => {
    if (!pick || !splash.isConnected) return;
    const desktopPath = pick.backdrop || pick.poster;
    const mobilePath = pick.poster || pick.backdrop;
    if (!desktopPath && !mobilePath) return;

    mediaWrap.replaceChildren();
    const pic = document.createElement('picture');
    pic.className = 'splash-picture';

    if (desktopPath) {
      const srcDesk = document.createElement('source');
      srcDesk.media = '(min-width: 900px) and (min-height: 600px)';
      srcDesk.srcset = TMDB_IMG + 'w1280' + desktopPath;
      pic.append(srcDesk);
    }

    const img = document.createElement('img');
    img.className = 'splash-bg-img';
    img.alt = '';
    img.decoding = 'async';
    img.addEventListener('load', () => img.classList.add('loaded'));
    img.src = TMDB_IMG + 'w780' + mobilePath;
    if (img.complete) img.classList.add('loaded');

    pic.append(img);
    mediaWrap.append(pic);
  };

  const cached = readSplashPool();
  if (cached) {
    const pick = cached.items[Math.floor(Math.random() * cached.items.length)];
    applyPick(pick);
  }

  // Оновлення пулу популярних фільмів TMDB (або перше завантаження)
  if (!cached || (Date.now() - (cached.ts || 0)) >= SPLASH_POOL_TTL) {
    tmdbFetch('/movie/popular?language=uk-UA&page=1')
      .then(d => {
        const items = (d.results || [])
          .filter(x => x && x.poster_path && x.backdrop_path)
          .map(x => ({ poster: x.poster_path, backdrop: x.backdrop_path }));
        if (!items.length) return;
        writeSplashPool(items);
        if (!cached) {
          const pick = items[Math.floor(Math.random() * items.length)];
          applyPick(pick);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }

  setTimeout(() => {
    splash.classList.add('hiding');
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (splash.isConnected) splash.remove();
    }, 420);
  }, SPLASH_DURATION_MS);
}

if (window.Capacitor && window.Capacitor.isNativePlatform && window.Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
  initColdSplash();
}
history.pushState({ mc: 0 }, '', location.pathname + location.search);
refresh();
routeFromHash();

      startOnboarding();
    }
  }, 800);
}

// ========== CAPACITOR BACK BUTTON (FIXED) ==========
(function () {
  const CapApp = window.Capacitor && window.Capacitor.Plugins && window.Capacitor.Plugins.App;
  if (!CapApp || !CapApp.addListener) {
    console.warn('Capacitor App plugin not found. Back button handling disabled.');
    return;
  }
  
  CapApp.addListener('backButton', () => {
    // 1. Модальні вікна та оверлеї
    if (document.getElementById('poster-zoom').classList.contains('open')) { closeImageZoom(); return; }
    if (document.getElementById('settings-modal').classList.contains('open')) { closeSettings(); return; }
    if (document.getElementById('auth-modal').style.display === 'flex') { closeAuthModal(); return; }
    

    // 2. Бокове меню
    if (document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.contains('open')) { closeSidebar(); return; }
    
    // 3. Автокомпліт та дропдаун фільтрів
    if (typeof filterDropdown !== 'undefined' && filterDropdown && !filterDropdown.classList.contains('hidden')) { hideFilterDropdown(); return; }
    if (document.querySelector('.nav-group.open')) { closeNavDropdowns(); return; }
    if (!autocomplete.classList.contains('hidden') && autocomplete.childElementCount) { hideAutocomplete(); return; }
    
    // 4. Стек карток (модалка тайтлу/актора)
    if (cardStack.length) { history.back(); return; }
    
    // 5. Таби (якщо не на головній)
    if (state.tab !== 'home') { setTab('home'); return; }
    
    // 6. Вихід з додатку (тільки з головної)
    CapApp.exitApp();
  });
})();

// Ініціалізація видимості пошуку
document.body.classList.toggle('no-search', !(state.tab === 'home' || state.tab === 'filter'));

function onFirebaseReadyUI() {
  const lb = document.getElementById('sidebar-logout-btn');
  if (lb && window.currentUser) {
    lb.title = (window.currentUser.email || 'Акаунт') + ' · Вийти з акаунту';
  }
}
window.addEventListener('firebase-ready', onFirebaseReadyUI);
if (window.firebaseReady) {
  onFirebaseReadyUI();
  syncFromCloud();
}
syncNavActive();

// Auto-sync when app comes to foreground
let _lastAutoSync = 0;
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
  if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && window.currentUser && window.firebaseReady) {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - _lastAutoSync > 30000) { // не частіше ніж раз на 30 секунд
      _lastAutoSync = now;
      pullFromCloud();
    }
  }
});

document.getElementById('settings-modal')?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  if (e.target.id === 'settings-modal') closeSettings();
});
document.getElementById('auth-modal')?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  if (e.target.id === 'auth-modal') closeAuthModal();
});

  FILE: ./android/app/src/main/java/com/mycinema/app/ExternalPlayerPlugin.java
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

package com.mycinema.app;

import android.content.ActivityNotFoundException;
import android.content.ComponentName;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.pm.PackageManager;
import android.net.Uri;
import org.json.JSONObject;
import com.getcapacitor.JSObject;
import com.getcapacitor.Plugin;
import com.getcapacitor.PluginCall;
import com.getcapacitor.PluginMethod;
import com.getcapacitor.annotation.CapacitorPlugin;

@CapacitorPlugin(name = "ExternalPlayer")
public class ExternalPlayerPlugin extends Plugin {

    private static final String[][] PLAYERS = {
        {"org.videolan.vlc", "org.videolan.vlc.gui.video.VideoPlayerActivity"},
        {"com.mxtech.videoplayer.ad", "com.mxtech.videoplayer.ad.ActivityScreen"},
        {"com.mxtech.videoplayer.pro", "com.mxtech.videoplayer.ActivityScreen"}
    };

    private Intent base(Uri uri, String title) {
        Intent i = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW);
        i.setDataAndType(uri, "video/*");
        i.putExtra("title", title);
        i.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TITLE, title);
        return i;
    }

    private void done(PluginCall call, String via) {
        JSObject r = new JSObject();
        r.put("via", via);
        call.resolve(r);
    }

    @PluginMethod
    public void open(PluginCall call) {
        String url = call.getString("url");
        String title = call.getString("title", "");

        if (url == null || url.isEmpty()) {
            call.reject("URL is required");
            return;
        }
        Uri uri = Uri.parse(url);

        // 1. Явний компонент (найнадійніше для ChromeOS)
        for (String[] p : PLAYERS) {
            try {
                Intent i = base(uri, title);
                i.setComponent(new ComponentName(p[0], p[1]));
                getActivity().startActivity(i);
                done(call, "component:" + p[0]);
                return;
            } catch (ActivityNotFoundException | SecurityException ignored) {}
        }

        // 2. Тільки пакет
        for (String[] p : PLAYERS) {
            try {
                Intent i = base(uri, title);
                i.setPackage(p[0]);
                getActivity().startActivity(i);
                done(call, "package:" + p[0]);
                return;
            } catch (ActivityNotFoundException | SecurityException ignored) {}
        }

        // 3. Системний Chooser
        try {
            getActivity().startActivity(Intent.createChooser(base(uri, title), "Open with"));
            done(call, "chooser");
        } catch (ActivityNotFoundException e) {
            call.reject("No video player found", e);
        }
    }

    @PluginMethod
    public void openLampa(PluginCall call) {
        final String lampaPkg = "top.rootu.lampa";
        final String lampaActivity = "top.rootu.lampa.MainActivity";

        PackageManager pm = getContext().getPackageManager();
        try {
            pm.getPackageInfo(lampaPkg, 0);
        } catch (PackageManager.NameNotFoundException e) {
            call.reject("NOT_INSTALLED");
            return;
        }

        Object rawId = call.getData().opt("id");
        String id = (rawId != null && rawId != JSONObject.NULL) ? String.valueOf(rawId).trim() : "";
        String rawType = call.getString("type", "movie");
        String media = "tv".equals(rawType) ? "tv" : "movie";

        if (!id.isEmpty()) {
            Uri tmdbUri = Uri.parse("https://www.themoviedb.org/" + media + "/" + id);

            // 1. Явний компонент Lampa (ChromeOS / ARCVM)
            try {
                Intent i = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, tmdbUri);
                i.setComponent(new ComponentName(lampaPkg, lampaActivity));
                i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
                getActivity().startActivity(i);
                done(call, "component:tmdb");
                return;
            } catch (ActivityNotFoundException | SecurityException ignored) {}

            // 2. Пакет Lampa
            try {
                Intent i = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, tmdbUri);
                i.setPackage(lampaPkg);
                i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
                getActivity().startActivity(i);
                done(call, "package:tmdb");
                return;
            } catch (ActivityNotFoundException | SecurityException ignored) {}
        }

        // 3. Fallback: просто відкрити Lampa
        Intent launch = pm.getLaunchIntentForPackage(lampaPkg);
        if (launch != null) {
            try {
                launch.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
                getActivity().startActivity(launch);
                done(call, "launch-only");
                return;
            } catch (ActivityNotFoundException | SecurityException ignored) {}
        }

        call.reject("OPEN_FAILED");
    }
}

public class MainActivity extends BridgeActivity {

        registerPlugin(ExternalPlayerPlugin.class);
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
    }
}

package com.getcapacitor.myapp;

import static org.junit.Assert.*;

import android.content.Context;
import androidx.test.ext.junit.runners.AndroidJUnit4;
import androidx.test.platform.app.InstrumentationRegistry;
import org.junit.Test;
import org.junit.runner.RunWith;

/**
 * Instrumented test, which will execute on an Android device.
 *
 * @see <a href="http://d.android.com/tools/testing">Testing documentation</a>
 */
@RunWith(AndroidJUnit4.class)
public class ExampleInstrumentedTest {

    @Test
    public void useAppContext() throws Exception {
        // Context of the app under test.
        Context appContext = InstrumentationRegistry.getInstrumentation().getTargetContext();

        assertEquals("com.getcapacitor.app", appContext.getPackageName());
    }
}

  FILE: ./android/app/src/test/java/com/getcapacitor/myapp/ExampleUnitTest.java
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

package com.getcapacitor.myapp;

import static org.junit.Assert.*;

import org.junit.Test;

/**
 * Example local unit test, which will execute on the development machine (host).
 *
 * @see <a href="http://d.android.com/tools/testing">Testing documentation</a>
 */
public class ExampleUnitTest {

    @Test
    public void addition_isCorrect() throws Exception {
        assertEquals(4, 2 + 2);
    }
}


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  FILE: ./.github/workflows/build.yml
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

name: Build Android APK
on: [push, workflow_dispatch]

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout Code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: '22'

      - name: Setup Java
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: 'temurin'
          java-version: '21'

      - name: Cache Gradle dependencies
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: |
            ~/.gradle/caches
            ~/.gradle/wrapper
          key: ${{ runner.os }}-gradle-${{ hashFiles('**/*.gradle*', '**/gradle-wrapper.properties') }}
          restore-keys: |
            ${{ runner.os }}-gradle-

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm install

      - name: Prepare Web Folder
        run: |
          mkdir -p www
          cp index.html www/
          cp styles.css www/
          cp manifest.json www/ || true
          cp -r src www/

      - name: Capacitor Sync
        run: npx cap sync android

      - name: Build APK
        run: |
          cd android
          chmod +x gradlew
          ./gradlew assembleDebug

      - name: Create Release and Upload APK
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
        with:
          tag_name: build-${{ github.run_number }}
          name: MyCinema Build ${{ github.run_number }}
          files: android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk
          draft: false
          prerelease: false
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
